- delta use_nuke (replay vs prev): mean -67.6, median -75, std 24.3, min -98, max 10 (n=336)
- delta nuke     (replay vs prev): mean -51.8, median -55, std 32.3, min -100, max 20 (n=336)
- use_nuke deviation (replay vs original): mean -83.4, median -90, std 20.5, min -100, max -10 (n=336)
- nuke     deviation (replay vs original): mean -57.9, median -60, std 30.8, min -100, max 0 (n=336)

## Deepseek-3.2-Simple - Turn 358 - retry 3

**Deviation (replay vs original):** use_nuke `-20`, nuke `-30`

- Original: use_nuke 50 => 80 (Δ +30), nuke 50 => 100 (Δ +50)
> Prioritize nuclear deterrence and naval/air defense to counter India's massive military threat. Maximize espionage to protect against enemy spies and diplomacy to secure city-state alliances for the upcoming World Congress vote. Maintain strong economic and scientific output to support military production and technological edge.

- Replay: use_nuke 50 => 60 (Δ +10), nuke 50 => 70 (Δ +20)
> Continuing UnitedNations grand strategy for diplomatic victory. Increased Nuke from 50→70 and UseNuke from 50→60 to establish credible nuclear deterrence against India's nuclear arsenal and massive military (17,888 strength). Increased Offense from 40→50 to support pushing back India's naval incursions near our territory. Increased Spaceship from 50→60 as backup victory path since we're approaching Information Era. All other priorities maintained - Diplomacy, Naval, Science, Gold, and Production remain at maximum for city-state competition and UN construction.
